			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_EC_in_werner3 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { cwec_q_HM = 0.08 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { cwec_q_ZI = -0.5 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { cwec_q_PP = -0.05 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { cwec_q_ST = -0.02 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { cwec_q_WE = 0.05 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_EC_in_charter }
			add_to_temp_variable = { cwec_q_ST = 0.02 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { cwec_q_DD = 0.01 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { cwec_q_SK = 0.06 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { cwec_q_ZS = -0.02 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_EC_in_airbus }
			add_to_temp_variable = { cwec_q_ZS = 0.02 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { cwec_q_BK = 0.02 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_EC_in_ariane }
			add_to_temp_variable = { cwec_q_BK = 0.01 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = cwec_q_HM tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_trade_opinion_factor }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = cwec_q_ZS tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_civilian_industry_tax_modifier }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = cwec_q_ZI tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_interest_rate_multiplier_modifier }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = cwec_q_WE tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_mdcw_cw_market_exposure }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = cwec_q_ST tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_stability_factor }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = cwec_q_PP tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_political_power_factor }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = cwec_q_KG tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_consumer_goods_factor }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = cwec_q_AK tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_total_workforce_modifier }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = cwec_q_POL tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_police_cost_multiplier_modifier }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = cwec_q_SK tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_social_cost_multiplier_modifier }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = cwec_q_DD tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_democratic_drift }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = cwec_q_BK tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier }

COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_usa = {
	effect_tooltip = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_if_accept
		GRE = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_open }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_quote_GRE }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = GRE.gdp_total }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.01 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_gre
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = -0.1 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_training_time_factor }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = -0.1 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_foreign_influence_defense_modifier }
			add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_GRE_research_0 bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 category = CAT_infantry }
			add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_GRE_research_1 bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 category = CAT_aircraft }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = GRE.gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = 0.00012 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_display = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_income_tt }
		}
		USA = {
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_usa
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.03 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_navy_org_factor }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.01 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = GRE.gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = 0.00012 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_display = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense_tt }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_usa = {
	clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_open
	USA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_pending clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_consent }
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_offer_usa = {
	COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_usa = yes
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_can_offer_usa = yes }
			GRE = {
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_quote_GRE = GRE.gdp_total }
				multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_quote_GRE = 0.01 }
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_ever_offered
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_open value = 1 days = 90 }
			USA = {
				set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_pending value = 1 days = 90 }
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_consent
				country_event = { id = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG.100 hours = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_try_commit_usa = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_can_commit = yes }
		GRE = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_quote_GRE }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_gre
			add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_GRE_research_0 bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 category = CAT_infantry }
			add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_GRE_research_1 bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 category = CAT_aircraft }
		}
		USA = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_usa }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_signed
		USA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_signed }
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_usa = yes
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accept_usa = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_pending
			GRE = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_open
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_eligible = yes
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_funded = yes
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_snapshot_valid = yes
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_consent
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_try_commit_usa = yes }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_decline_usa = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_pending
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_open }
		}
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_usa = yes set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_declined }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_terminate_usa = {
	GRE = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_signed }
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_signed
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_active
			GRE = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_active remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_gre }
			USA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_active remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_usa }
			USA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_signed }
		}
	}
}

			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.08 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_offices_productivity }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_start_restricted_european_association }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = GRE.gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = 0.00004 }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_members = global.cwec_members^num }
			if = {
				limit = {
					GRE = {
						is_in_array = { array = global.cwec_members value = GRE }
					}
				}
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_members = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_signed }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_members = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_members^num }
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_members }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_display = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense_tt }
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.cwec_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { tag = GRE }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_member
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.01 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_office_park_income_tax_modifier }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.005 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier }
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = GRE.gdp_total }
				multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = 0.00004 }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_display = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_income_tt }
			}
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_association = {
	clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_open
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_members
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_consent
	}
}

COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_refresh_association = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_review
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_signed }
		}
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_review
		clear_array = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_members
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.cwec_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { tag = GRE }
				}
				add_to_array = { GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_members = THIS }
			}
		}
		clear_array = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_laws
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = COLDWAR_EC_done_A10 }
			add_to_array = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_laws = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = COLDWAR_EC_done_A9 }
			add_to_array = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_laws = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = COLDWAR_EC_done_P3 }
			add_to_array = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_laws = 3 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = COLDWAR_EC_done_P4 }
			add_to_array = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_laws = 4 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = COLDWAR_EC_done_P5 }
			add_to_array = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_laws = 5 }

			limit = { has_global_flag = COLDWAR_EC_done_P8 }
			add_to_array = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_laws = 6 }

		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_native_law_0 = cwec_m_AK }

		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_native_law_10 = cwec_m_KG }

COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_review_association = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_open
			NOT = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_snapshot_valid = yes }
		}
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_association = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_review
		country_event = { id = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG.901 hours = 1 }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_try_commit_association = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_can_commit = yes }
		GRE = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_quote_GRE }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_gre
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_start_restricted_european_association }
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = { array = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_members add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_member }
		COLDWAR_EC_become_associate = yes
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_EC_frozen
		COLDWAR_EC_refresh_external_country = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_signed
		for_each_scope_loop = { array = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_members set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_signed }
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_association = yes
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accept_association = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_pending
			GRE = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_open
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_eligible = yes
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_funded = yes
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_snapshot_valid = yes
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_consent
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_try_commit_association = yes }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_decline_association = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_pending
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_open }
		}
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_association = yes set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_declined }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_terminate_association = {
	GRE = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_signed }
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_signed
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_active
			GRE = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_active remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_gre }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_members
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_active
				remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_member
			}
			for_each_scope_loop = { array = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_members clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_signed }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_accession = {
	effect_tooltip = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_if_accept
		GRE = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_open }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_quote_GRE }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = GRE.gdp_total }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.03 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_gre
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.08 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_production_speed_industrial_complex_factor }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.05 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.08 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_offices_productivity }
			add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_GRE_research_1 bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 category = CAT_industry }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = GRE.gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = 0.00004 }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_members = global.cwec_members^num }
			if = {
				limit = {
					GRE = {
						is_in_array = { array = global.cwec_members value = GRE }
					}
				}
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_members = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_signed }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_members = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_members^num }
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_members }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_display = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense_tt }
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.cwec_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { tag = GRE }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_member
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.01 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_office_park_income_tax_modifier }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.005 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier }
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = GRE.gdp_total }
				multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = 0.00004 }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_display = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_income_tt }
			}
		}
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_native_eu_laws = yes }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_accession = {
	clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_open
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_members
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_consent
	}
}

COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_refresh_accession = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_review
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_signed }
		}
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_review
		clear_array = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_members
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.cwec_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { tag = GRE }
				}
				add_to_array = { GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_members = THIS }
			}
		}
		clear_array = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_laws
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = COLDWAR_EC_done_A10 }

		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = COLDWAR_EC_done_A9 }

		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = COLDWAR_EC_done_P3 }

COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_review_accession = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_open
			NOT = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_snapshot_valid = yes }
		}
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_accession = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_review
		country_event = { id = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG.902 hours = 1 }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_try_commit_accession = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_can_commit = yes }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_signed
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_active
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_gre
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_members
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_member
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_signed
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_active
		}
		GRE = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_quote_GRE }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_gre
			COLDWAR_EC_join_member = yes
			add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_GRE_research_1 bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 category = CAT_industry }
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = { array = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_members add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_member }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_signed
		for_each_scope_loop = { array = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_members set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_signed }
		COLDWAR_EC_refresh_country = yes
		COLDWAR_EC_recount = yes
		COLDWAR_EC_budget_recompute = yes
		COLDWAR_EC_refresh_all = yes
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_accession = yes
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accept_accession = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_pending
			GRE = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_open
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_eligible = yes
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_funded = yes
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_snapshot_valid = yes
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_consent
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_try_commit_accession = yes }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_decline_accession = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_pending
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_open }
		}
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_accession = yes set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_declined }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_terminate_accession = {
	GRE = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_signed }
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_signed
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_active
			GRE = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_active remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_gre }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_members
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_active
				remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_member
			}
			for_each_scope_loop = { array = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_members clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_signed }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_yugoslavia = {
	effect_tooltip = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_if_accept
		GRE = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_yugoslavia_open }

				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_yugoslavia_quote_GRE }

				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			else = {

				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.015 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes

			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.02 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier }

			140 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
			}
		}
		SER = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_yugoslavia_open }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_yugoslavia_quote_SER }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.01 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_yugoslavia_ser
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.02 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.04 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_offices_productivity }

			}
		}

COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_offer_germany = {
	COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_germany = yes
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_can_offer_germany = yes }
			GRE = {
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_quote_GRE = GRE.gdp_total }
				multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_quote_GRE = 0.01 }
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_ever_offered
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_open value = 1 days = 90 }
			GER = {
				set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_pending value = 1 days = 90 }
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_consent
				country_event = { id = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG.108 hours = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_try_commit_germany = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_can_commit = yes }
		GRE = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_quote_GRE }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_gre
			add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_GRE_research_0 bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 category = CAT_industry }
		}
		GER = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_ger }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_signed
		GER = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_signed }
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_germany = yes
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accept_germany = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_pending
			GRE = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_open
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_eligible = yes
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_funded = yes
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_snapshot_valid = yes
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_consent
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_try_commit_germany = yes }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_decline_germany = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_pending
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_open }
		}
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_germany = yes set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_declined }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_terminate_germany = {
	GRE = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_signed }
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_signed
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_active
			GRE = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_active remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_gre }
			GER = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_active remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_ger }
			GER = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_signed }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_soviet = {
	effect_tooltip = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_if_accept
		GRE = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_soviet_open }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_soviet_quote_GRE }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = GRE.gdp_total }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.015 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_soviet_gre
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.1 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_civilian_factories_productivity }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = -0.1 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_foreign_influence_defense_modifier }
			add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_soviet_GRE_research_0 bonus = 0.5 uses = 2 category = CAT_industry }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = GRE.gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = 0.00008 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_display = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense_tt }
		}
		SOV = {
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_soviet_sov
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.01 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_research_speed_factor }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.01 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = GRE.gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = 0.00008 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_display = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_income_tt }
		}
	}
}

		CYP = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_settlement_quote_CYP }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }

			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_settlement_cyp
		}
		TUR = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_settlement_quote_TUR }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_settlement_tur
		}
		ENG = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_settlement_eng }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_settlement_signed
		CYP = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_settlement_signed }
		TUR = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_settlement_signed }
		ENG = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_settlement_signed }
		CYP = { COLDWAR_CYP_R2_resolve_cyprus_crisis = yes set_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_security_terms_accepted }

	}

COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_enosis_full = {
	effect_tooltip = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_if_accept
		GRE = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_open }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_quote_GRE }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = GRE.gdp_total }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.05 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_gre
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.05 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_war_support_factor }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = -0.1 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_stability_factor }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = 0.1 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = -0.08 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_office_park_income_tax_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = -0.1 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_foreign_influence_defense_modifier }
			add_political_power = -150
			annex_country = { target = CYP transfer_troops = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = GRE.gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly = 0.0003 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_display = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_weekly tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense_tt }
		}
		CYP = {  }
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_if_refuse
		GRE = {
			create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = CYP }
		}
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_union_ledger = yes }
	}
}

COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accept_enosis_full = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_pending
			GRE = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_open
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_eligible = yes
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_funded = yes
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_snapshot_valid = yes
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_consent
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_open_enosis_warning = yes }
		GRE = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					CYP = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_consent }
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_warning_closed
					NOT = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_union_quote_valid = yes }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_round_pending }
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_round_pending
				country_event = { id = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG.809 hours = 1 }
			}
		}
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_try_commit_enosis_full = yes }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_decline_enosis_full = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_pending
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_open }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = CYP
				GRE = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_refusal_wargoal_issued }
					COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_territory_valid = yes
				}
			}
			GRE = {
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_refusal_wargoal_issued
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_refusal_enosis_full
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_wargoal_against = { target = CYP type = annex_everything }
						}
					}
					create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = CYP }
				}
			}
		}
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_enosis_full = yes set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_declined }
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_pending
			GRE = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_open
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_eligible = yes
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_funded = yes
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_snapshot_valid = yes
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_consent
		GRE = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					CYP = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_consent }
					TUR = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_consent }
					ENG = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_consent }
					NCY = {
						OR = {
							NOT = {
								146 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = NCY }
							}
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_consent
						}
					}
					NOT = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_union_quote_valid = yes }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_round_pending }
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_round_pending
				country_event = { id = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG.811 hours = 1 }
			}
		}
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_try_commit_enosis_south = yes }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_decline_enosis_south = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_pending
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_open }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = CYP
				GRE = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_refusal_wargoal_issued }
					COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_territory_valid = yes
				}
			}
			GRE = {
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_refusal_wargoal_issued
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_refusal_enosis_south
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_wargoal_against = { target = CYP type = take_state_focus }
						}
					}
					create_wargoal = {
						type = take_state_focus
						target = CYP
						generator = { 145 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_enosis_south = yes set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_declined }
	}
}

			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = -0.05 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_training_time_factor }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview = -0.05 tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_mod_foreign_influence_defense_modifier }

COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_try_commit_cyprus_defence = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_can_commit = yes }
		GRE = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_quote_GRE }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_gre
			army_experience = 25
		}
		CYP = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_cyp }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_signed
		CYP = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_signed }
		CYP = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_greek_network_withdrawn clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_greek_network_active }
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_close_cyprus_defence = yes
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reconcile = yes
	}
}

			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_pending
			GRE = {

				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_eligible = yes

				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_snapshot_valid = yes

		}

COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_terminate_cyprus_defence = {
	GRE = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_signed }
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_signed
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_active
			GRE = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_active remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_gre }
			CYP = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_active remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_cyp }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_access_cyp_to_gre }
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_access_cyp_to_gre
				diplomatic_relation = { country = CYP relation = military_access active = no }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_access_gre_to_cyp }
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_access_gre_to_cyp
				CYP = {
					diplomatic_relation = { country = GRE relation = military_access active = no }
				}
			}
			CYP = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_signed }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_deliver_two_divisions = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = CYP
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reinforcements_pending
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reinforcements_consent
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reinforcements_delivered }
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reinforcements_open COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reinforcements_eligible = yes }
		}
		GRE = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_free_manpower = manpower_k }
			multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_free_manpower = 1000 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_reserve_infantry_weapons_type = num_equipment@infantry_weapons_type }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_reserve_L_AT_Equipment = num_equipment@L_AT_Equipment }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_reserve_AA_Equipment = num_equipment@AA_Equipment }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_reserve_cnc_equipment_type = num_equipment@cnc_equipment_type }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_divisions_before = num_divisions }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_divisions_expected = num_divisions }
		add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_divisions_expected = 2 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_free_manpower = manpower_k }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_free_manpower = 1000 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_manpower = deployed_army_manpower_k }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_reserve_infantry_weapons_type = num_equipment@infantry_weapons_type }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_infantry_weapons_type = num_equipment_in_armies@infantry_weapons_type }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_reserve_L_AT_Equipment = num_equipment@L_AT_Equipment }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_L_AT_Equipment = num_equipment_in_armies@L_AT_Equipment }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_reserve_AA_Equipment = num_equipment@AA_Equipment }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_AA_Equipment = num_equipment_in_armies@AA_Equipment }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_reserve_cnc_equipment_type = num_equipment@cnc_equipment_type }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_cnc_equipment_type = num_equipment_in_armies@cnc_equipment_type }
		division_template = {
			name = "Greek-Supplied Light Infantry"
			is_locked = yes
			regiments = {
				L_Inf_Bat = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				L_Inf_Bat = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				L_Inf_Bat = { x = 0 y = 2 }
				L_Inf_Bat = { x = 1 y = 0 }
				L_Inf_Bat = { x = 1 y = 1 }
				L_Inf_Bat = { x = 1 y = 2 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				145 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = CYP }
				controls_province = 11984
			}
			145 = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"1st Hellenic-Cypriot Infantry\" division_template = \"Greek-Supplied Light Infantry\" start_experience_factor = 0.3 start_equipment_factor = 1 start_manpower_factor = 1"
					owner = CYP
					prioritize_location = 11984
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
					id = 1970110101
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			146 = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"1st Hellenic-Cypriot Infantry\" division_template = \"Greek-Supplied Light Infantry\" start_experience_factor = 0.3 start_equipment_factor = 1 start_manpower_factor = 1"
					owner = CYP
					prioritize_location = 7193
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
					id = 1970110101
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				146 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = CYP }
				controls_province = 7193
			}
			146 = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"2nd Hellenic-Cypriot Infantry\" division_template = \"Greek-Supplied Light Infantry\" start_experience_factor = 0.3 start_equipment_factor = 1 start_manpower_factor = 1"
					owner = CYP
					prioritize_location = 7193
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
					id = 1970110102
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			145 = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"2nd Hellenic-Cypriot Infantry\" division_template = \"Greek-Supplied Light Infantry\" start_experience_factor = 0.3 start_equipment_factor = 1 start_manpower_factor = 1"
					owner = CYP
					prioritize_location = 11984
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
					id = 1970110102
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_manpower_delta = deployed_army_manpower_k }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_manpower_delta = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_manpower }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_delta_infantry_weapons_type = num_equipment_in_armies@infantry_weapons_type }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_delta_infantry_weapons_type = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_infantry_weapons_type }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_delta_L_AT_Equipment = num_equipment_in_armies@L_AT_Equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_delta_L_AT_Equipment = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_L_AT_Equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_delta_AA_Equipment = num_equipment_in_armies@AA_Equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_delta_AA_Equipment = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_AA_Equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_delta_cnc_equipment_type = num_equipment_in_armies@cnc_equipment_type }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_delta_cnc_equipment_type = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_cnc_equipment_type }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = num_divisions value = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_divisions_expected compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_manpower_delta value = 2.4 compare = equals }
				GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reinforcements_eligible = yes }
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_delta_infantry_weapons_type value = 2400 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_delta_L_AT_Equipment value = 240 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_delta_AA_Equipment value = 240 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_army_delta_cnc_equipment_type value = 240 compare = equals }
			}
			GRE = {
				add_manpower = -2400
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_weapons_type amount = -2400 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = L_AT_Equipment amount = -240 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = AA_Equipment amount = -240 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = cnc_equipment_type amount = -240 }
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_manpower_delta = manpower_k }
				multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_manpower_delta = -1000 }
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_manpower_delta = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_free_manpower }
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_infantry_weapons_type = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_reserve_infantry_weapons_type }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_infantry_weapons_type = num_equipment@infantry_weapons_type }
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_L_AT_Equipment = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_reserve_L_AT_Equipment }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_L_AT_Equipment = num_equipment@L_AT_Equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_AA_Equipment = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_reserve_AA_Equipment }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_AA_Equipment = num_equipment@AA_Equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_cnc_equipment_type = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_reserve_cnc_equipment_type }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_cnc_equipment_type = num_equipment@cnc_equipment_type }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					GRE = {
						check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_manpower_delta value = 2400 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_infantry_weapons_type value = 2400 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_L_AT_Equipment value = 240 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_AA_Equipment value = 240 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_cnc_equipment_type value = 240 compare = equals }
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reinforcements_delivered
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_CYP_reinforcements_delivered
				GRE = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_CYP_reinforcements_delivered }
			}
			else = {
				delete_unit = { id = 1970110101 disband = no }
				delete_unit = { id = 1970110102 disband = no }
				GRE = {
					add_manpower = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_manpower_delta
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_infantry_weapons_type value = 0 compare = equals }
							}
						}
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_weapons_type amount = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_infantry_weapons_type }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_L_AT_Equipment value = 0 compare = equals }
							}
						}
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = L_AT_Equipment amount = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_L_AT_Equipment }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_AA_Equipment value = 0 compare = equals }
							}
						}
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = AA_Equipment amount = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_AA_Equipment }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_cnc_equipment_type value = 0 compare = equals }
							}
						}
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = cnc_equipment_type amount = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donor_delta_cnc_equipment_type }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			delete_unit = { id = 1970110101 disband = no }
			delete_unit = { id = 1970110102 disband = no }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_manpower_restore = manpower_k }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_manpower_restore = -1000 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_manpower_restore = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_free_manpower }
		add_manpower = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_manpower_restore
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_infantry_weapons_type = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_reserve_infantry_weapons_type }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_infantry_weapons_type = num_equipment@infantry_weapons_type }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_infantry_weapons_type value = 0 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_weapons_type amount = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_infantry_weapons_type }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_L_AT_Equipment = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_reserve_L_AT_Equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_L_AT_Equipment = num_equipment@L_AT_Equipment }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_L_AT_Equipment value = 0 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = L_AT_Equipment amount = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_L_AT_Equipment }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_AA_Equipment = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_reserve_AA_Equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_AA_Equipment = num_equipment@AA_Equipment }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_AA_Equipment value = 0 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = AA_Equipment amount = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_AA_Equipment }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_cnc_equipment_type = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_reserve_cnc_equipment_type }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_cnc_equipment_type = num_equipment@cnc_equipment_type }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_cnc_equipment_type value = 0 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = cnc_equipment_type amount = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_donation_restore_cnc_equipment_type }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_count_reserve_aircraft = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reserve_aircraft = 0 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reserve_aircraft = CYP.num_equipment@cv_medium_plane_airframe }
	add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reserve_aircraft = CYP.num_equipment@cv_small_plane_airframe }
	add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reserve_aircraft = CYP.num_equipment@large_plane_airframe }
	add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reserve_aircraft = CYP.num_equipment@medium_plane_airframe }
	add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reserve_aircraft = CYP.num_equipment@mothership_equipment }
	add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reserve_aircraft = CYP.num_equipment@small_plane_airframe }
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_capture_union_quote = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_GRE_treasury = GRE.treasury }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_GRE_debt = GRE.debt }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_GRE_int_investments = GRE.int_investments }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_CYP_treasury = CYP.treasury }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_CYP_debt = CYP.debt }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_CYP_int_investments = CYP.int_investments }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_CYP_num_divisions = CYP.num_divisions }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_CYP_num_ships = CYP.num_ships }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_CYP_num_deployed_planes = CYP.num_deployed_planes }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_air_cv_medium_plane_airframe = CYP.num_equipment@cv_medium_plane_airframe }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_air_cv_small_plane_airframe = CYP.num_equipment@cv_small_plane_airframe }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_air_large_plane_airframe = CYP.num_equipment@large_plane_airframe }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_air_medium_plane_airframe = CYP.num_equipment@medium_plane_airframe }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_air_mothership_equipment = CYP.num_equipment@mothership_equipment }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_air_small_plane_airframe = CYP.num_equipment@small_plane_airframe }
	COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_count_reserve_aircraft = yes
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_CYP_reserve_aircraft = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reserve_aircraft }
	every_country = {
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_held_gre = given_bonds@GRE }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_held_cyp = given_bonds@CYP }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_quote_cyp_held = CYP.given_bonds@THIS }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_union_ledger = {
	effect_tooltip = {
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_ledger = CYP.treasury tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_treasury_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_ledger = CYP.debt tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_debt_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_ledger = CYP.int_investments tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_investments_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_ledger = CYP.num_divisions tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_divisions_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_ledger = CYP.num_ships tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ships_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_ledger = CYP.num_deployed_planes tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_aircraft_tt }
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_count_reserve_aircraft = yes
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_ledger = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reserve_aircraft tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reserve_aircraft_tt }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = CYP.treasury }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { debt_change = CYP.debt }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { debt_change = GRE.given_bonds@CYP }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { debt_change = CYP.given_bonds@GRE }
		modify_debt_effect = yes
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { given_bonds@CYP > 0 }
					check_variable = { CYP.given_bonds@THIS > 0 }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_ledger = given_bonds@CYP tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_bonds_owed_tt }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_ledger = CYP.given_bonds@THIS tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_bonds_held_tt }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_commit_enosis_full_union = {
	set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_scripted_union_in_progress
	COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_migrate_union_ledger = yes
	annex_country = { target = CYP transfer_troops = yes }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { country_exists = CYP }
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_retained = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_signed
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_full_committed
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_incorporated
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_integration_clock
		145 = { set_state_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_union_recorded }
		146 = { set_state_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_union_recorded }
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cleanup_independent_cyprus = yes
		update_money_dirty_variable = yes
		ingame_update_setup = yes
		CYP = {  }
	}
	else = {
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_rollback_union_ledger = yes
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_gre
		add_political_power = 150
	}
	clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_scripted_union_in_progress
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_commit_enosis_south_union = {
	set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_scripted_union_in_progress
	COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_migrate_union_ledger = yes
	annex_country = { target = CYP transfer_troops = yes }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { country_exists = CYP }
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_retained = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_signed
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_south_committed
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_incorporated
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_integration_clock
		145 = { set_state_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_union_recorded }
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cleanup_independent_cyprus = yes
		update_money_dirty_variable = yes
		ingame_update_setup = yes
		CYP = {  }
		TUR = {
			add_political_power = -25
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = GRE modifier = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_TUR_opinion }
		}
		ENG = {
			add_political_power = -10
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = GRE modifier = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_ENG_opinion }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				146 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = NCY }
			}
			NCY = {
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = GRE modifier = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_NCY_opinion }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_rollback_union_ledger = yes
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_gre
		add_political_power = 100
	}
	clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_scripted_union_in_progress
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_capture_union_ledgers = {
	GRE = {
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_treasury = treasury }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_debt = debt }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_int_investments = int_investments }
	}
	CYP = {
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_treasury = treasury }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_debt = debt }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_int_investments = int_investments }
	}
	every_country = {
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_given_GRE = given_bonds@GRE }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_given_CYP = given_bonds@CYP }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_CYP_given = CYP.given_bonds@THIS }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_migrate_union_ledger = {
	set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = CYP.treasury }
	modify_treasury_effect = yes
	set_temp_variable = { debt_change = CYP.debt }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { debt_change = given_bonds@CYP }
	subtract_from_variable = { debt = CYP.given_bonds@GRE }
	modify_debt_effect = yes
	add_to_variable = { int_investments = CYP.int_investments }
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { tag = GRE }
			NOT = { tag = CYP }
		}
		add_to_variable = { given_bonds@GRE = given_bonds@CYP }
		set_variable = { given_bonds@CYP = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_incoming_holding = CYP.given_bonds@THIS }
		GRE = {
			add_to_variable = { given_bonds@PREV = PREV.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_incoming_holding }
		}
		CYP = {
			set_variable = { given_bonds@PREV = 0 }
		}
		calculate_new_bonds_held = yes
		calculate_new_bonds_owed = yes
		update_money_dirty_variable = yes
	}
	set_variable = { given_bonds@CYP = 0 }
	CYP = {
		set_variable = { given_bonds@GRE = 0 }
		set_variable = { treasury = 0 }
		set_variable = { debt = 0 }
		set_variable = { int_investments = 0 }
	}
	calculate_new_bonds_held = yes
	calculate_new_bonds_owed = yes
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_migrate_conquest_bonds = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { tag = GRE }
			NOT = { tag = CYP }
		}
		add_to_variable = { given_bonds@GRE = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_given_CYP }
		set_variable = { given_bonds@CYP = 0 }
		GRE = {
			add_to_variable = { given_bonds@PREV = PREV.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_CYP_given }
		}
		CYP = {
			set_variable = { given_bonds@PREV = 0 }
		}
		calculate_new_bonds_held = yes
		calculate_new_bonds_owed = yes
		update_money_dirty_variable = yes
	}
	set_variable = { given_bonds@CYP = 0 }
	CYP = {
		set_variable = { given_bonds@GRE = 0 }
	}
	calculate_new_bonds_held = yes
	calculate_new_bonds_owed = yes
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_rollback_union_ledger = {
	GRE = {
		set_variable = { treasury = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_treasury }
		set_variable = { debt = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_debt }
		set_variable = { int_investments = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_int_investments }
	}
	CYP = {
		set_variable = { treasury = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_treasury }
		set_variable = { debt = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_debt }
		set_variable = { int_investments = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_int_investments }
	}
	every_country = {
		set_variable = { given_bonds@GRE = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_given_GRE }
		set_variable = { given_bonds@CYP = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_given_CYP }
		CYP = {
			set_variable = { given_bonds@PREV = PREV.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_CYP_given }
		}
		calculate_new_bonds_held = yes
		calculate_new_bonds_owed = yes
		update_money_dirty_variable = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_open_enosis_warning = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_open
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_warning_used }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_warning_used
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_warning_open
		TUR = {
			country_event = { id = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG.801 hours = 1 }
		}
		country_event = { id = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG.802 days = 30 }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_separate_nato = {
	GRE = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = NATO_member }
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_nato_was_member
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_nato_separated
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_nato_was_member
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_nato_separated
			NATO_leave = yes
		}
	}
	TUR = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = NATO_member }
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_nato_was_member
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_nato_separated
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_nato_was_member
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_nato_separated
			NATO_leave = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_escalate_enosis = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			TUR = { has_war_with = CYP has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_intervention }
			NOT = { has_war_with = TUR }
		}
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_separate_nato = yes
		add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = CYP enemy = TUR hostility_reason = asked_to_join }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_on_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			GRE = {
				has_war_with = CYP
				OR = {
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_refusal_enosis_full
						has_war_with_wargoal_against = { target = CYP type = annex_everything }
					}
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_refusal_enosis_south
						has_war_with_wargoal_against = { target = CYP type = take_state_focus }
					}
				}
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_refusal_wargoal_issued
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_refusal_war_recorded }
			}
		}
		GRE = {
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_refusal_war_recorded
			TUR = {
				country_event = { id = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG.808 hours = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_pre_annex = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = GRE
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_refusal_war_recorded
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_scripted_union_in_progress }
			}
			FROM = { tag = CYP }
		}
		GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_capture_union_ledgers = yes set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_conquest_snapshot_ready }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_on_annex = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = GRE
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_refusal_war_recorded
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_scripted_union_in_progress }
			}
			FROM = { tag = CYP }
		}
		GRE = {
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_conquest_native_annexed
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_integration_clock }
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_integration_clock
			}
		}
	}
}

			}

			GRE = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_active add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_gre }
			CYP = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_active add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_cyp }
			if = {
				limit = {
					CYP = {
						NOT = { has_military_access_to = GRE }
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_access_cyp_to_gre
				give_military_access = CYP
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_military_access_to = CYP }
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_access_gre_to_cyp
				CYP = { give_military_access = GRE }
			}
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_active
			GRE = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_active remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_gre }
			CYP = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_active remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_cyp }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_access_cyp_to_gre }
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_access_cyp_to_gre
				diplomatic_relation = { country = CYP relation = military_access active = no }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_access_gre_to_cyp }
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_access_gre_to_cyp
				CYP = {
					diplomatic_relation = { country = GRE relation = military_access active = no }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defences_signed
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defences_relations = yes
				CYP = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defences_signed }
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defences_active
			GRE = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defences_active }
			CYP = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defences_active }
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defences_active
			GRE = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defences_active }
			CYP = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defences_active }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_signed
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_integrated }
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_any_retained = yes
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_gre
		}
		else = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_gre }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_signed
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_integrated }
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_any_retained = yes
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_gre
		}
		else = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_gre }
		if = {
			limit = {
				TUR = {
					NOT = { has_war_with = CYP }
				}
			}
			TUR = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_intervention }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_war_paused
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_resume_used }
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_territory_valid = yes
				CYP = {
					NOT = { has_war_with = TUR }
				}
				NOT = { has_war_with = TUR }
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_resume_used
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_war_paused
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_warning_closed
			country_event = { id = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG.804 hours = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_refusal_war_recorded
				country_exists = CYP
				NOT = { has_war_with = CYP }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_conquest_native_annexed }
			}
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_refusal_war_recorded
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_conquest_snapshot_ready
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_refusal_enosis_full
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_conquest_snapshot_ready
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_conquest_native_annexed
				NOT = { country_exists = CYP }
				NOT = { has_war = yes }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_incorporated }
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_retained = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = CYP.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_treasury }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { debt_change = CYP.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_debt }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { debt_change = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_given_CYP }
			subtract_from_variable = { debt = CYP.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_given_GRE }
			modify_debt_effect = yes
			add_to_variable = { int_investments = CYP.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_int_investments }
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_migrate_conquest_bonds = yes
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.05 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_political_power = -150
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { treasury < 0 }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { debt_change = treasury }
				multiply_temp_variable = { debt_change = -1 }
				modify_debt_effect = yes
				set_variable = { treasury = 0 }
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_incorporated
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_full_committed
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_signed
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_gre
			145 = { set_state_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_union_recorded }
			146 = { set_state_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_union_recorded }
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cleanup_independent_cyprus = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_refusal_enosis_south
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_conquest_snapshot_ready
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_conquest_native_annexed
				NOT = { country_exists = CYP }
				NOT = { has_war = yes }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_incorporated }
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_retained = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = CYP.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_treasury }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { debt_change = CYP.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_debt }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { debt_change = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_given_CYP }
			subtract_from_variable = { debt = CYP.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_given_GRE }
			modify_debt_effect = yes
			add_to_variable = { int_investments = CYP.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_backup_int_investments }
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_migrate_conquest_bonds = yes
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.025 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_political_power = -100
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { treasury < 0 }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { debt_change = treasury }
				multiply_temp_variable = { debt_change = -1 }
				modify_debt_effect = yes
				set_variable = { treasury = 0 }
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_incorporated
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_south_committed
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_signed
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_gre
			145 = { set_state_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_union_recorded }
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cleanup_independent_cyprus = yes
		}
		GRE = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_nato_was_member
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_nato_separated
					GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_nato_readmission_clear = yes }
					NOT = { is_in_faction = yes }
				}
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3_MIL_restore_nato_membership = yes
				if = {
					limit = { has_idea = NATO_member }
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_nato_separated
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_nato_was_member
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_nato_separated
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_nato_was_member
				}
			}
		}
		TUR = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_nato_was_member
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_nato_separated
					GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_nato_readmission_clear = yes }
					NOT = { is_in_faction = yes }
				}
				COLDWAR_GRE_R3_MIL_restore_nato_membership = yes
				if = {
					limit = { has_idea = NATO_member }
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_nato_separated
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_nato_was_member
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_nato_separated
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_nato_was_member
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_integrated
				OR = {
					AND = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_full_committed COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_any_retained = yes }
					AND = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_south_committed COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_any_retained = yes }
				}
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cypriot_regional_representation
		}
		else = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cypriot_regional_representation }
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_calculate_income = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_income = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = GRE
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_relations = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00012 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_income = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = FRA
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_france_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_france_relations = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00008 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_income = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_relations = yes }
			is_in_array = { array = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_members value = THIS }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00004 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_income = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_relations = yes }
			is_in_array = { array = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_members value = THIS }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00004 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_income = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = ENG
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_britain_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_britain_relations = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00004 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_income = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = GER
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_relations = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00008 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_income = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = SOV
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_soviet_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_soviet_relations = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00008 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_income = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = CYP
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_trade_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_trade_relations = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00004 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_income = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	add_to_variable = { additional_income_rate = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_income }
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_calculate_expense = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = USA
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_usa_relations = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00012 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = GRE
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_france_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_france_relations = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00008 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = GRE
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_relations = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00004 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_association_members^num }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = GRE
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_relations = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00004 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_accession_members^num }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = GRE
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_britain_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_britain_relations = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00004 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = GRE
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_germany_relations = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00008 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = GRE
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_soviet_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_soviet_relations = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00008 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = GRE
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_trade_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_trade_relations = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00004 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = GRE has_idea = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_gre COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_any_retained = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.0003 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = GRE has_idea = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_gre COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_any_retained = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00015 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = GRE
			GRE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_active COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cyprus_defence_relations = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = GRE.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.0001 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = GRE
			has_idea = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_cypriot_regional_representation
			OR = {
				AND = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_full_committed COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_full_any_retained = yes }
				AND = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R2_enosis_south_committed COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_enosis_south_any_retained = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream = 0.00004 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_stream }
	}
	add_to_variable = { additional_expenses_rate = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_weekly_expense }
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_relationship_changed = {
	GRE = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reconcile = yes }
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_preview_community_budget = {
	effect_tooltip = {
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gdp = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_districts = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_pop = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_revenue = 0 }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.cwec_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = yes
					NOT = { tag = GRE }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gdp = gdp_total }
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_districts = agriculture_district_total }
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_pop = population_total_m }
