				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_other_payer_gdp = gdp_total }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_rebate > 0 }
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_rebate = 0.66 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = GRE.id value = global.cwec_rebate compare = equals }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_recv = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_rebate }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_pay > COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_recv }
					}
					clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_other_payer_gdp min = 0.001 }
					set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_x = GRE.gdp_total }
					divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_x = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_other_payer_gdp }
					multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_x = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_rebate }
					add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_pay = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_x }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.cwec_l1_target > 0 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_loan = global.cwec_l1_weekly }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_loan = 52 }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_lender_gdp = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gdp }
			if = {
				limit = {
					var:global.cwec_l1_target = { exists = yes }
				}
				var:global.cwec_l1_target = {
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_lender_gdp = gdp_total }
				}
			}
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_lender_gdp min = 0.001 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = GRE.id value = global.cwec_l1_target compare = equals }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_pay = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_loan }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_x = GRE.gdp_total }
				divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_x = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_lender_gdp }
				multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_x = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_loan }
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_recv = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_x }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.cwec_l2_target > 0 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_loan = global.cwec_l2_weekly }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_loan = 52 }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_lender_gdp = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gdp }
			if = {
				limit = {
					var:global.cwec_l2_target = { exists = yes }
				}
				var:global.cwec_l2_target = {
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_lender_gdp = gdp_total }
				}
			}
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_lender_gdp min = 0.001 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = GRE.id value = global.cwec_l2_target compare = equals }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_pay = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_loan }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_x = GRE.gdp_total }
				divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_x = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_lender_gdp }
				multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_x = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_loan }
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_recv = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_x }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.cwec_h1_target > 0 }
				check_variable = { global.cwec_h1_remit > 0 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = GRE.id value = global.cwec_h1_by compare = equals }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_pay = global.cwec_h1_remit }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = GRE.id value = global.cwec_h1_target compare = equals }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_recv = global.cwec_h1_remit }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.cwec_h2_target > 0 }
				check_variable = { global.cwec_h2_remit > 0 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = GRE.id value = global.cwec_h2_by compare = equals }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_pay = global.cwec_h2_remit }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = GRE.id value = global.cwec_h2_target compare = equals }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_recv = global.cwec_h2_remit }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.cwec_h3_target > 0 }
				check_variable = { global.cwec_h3_remit > 0 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = GRE.id value = global.cwec_h3_by compare = equals }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_pay = global.cwec_h3_remit }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = GRE.id value = global.cwec_h3_target compare = equals }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_recv = global.cwec_h3_remit }
			}
		}
		divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_pay = 52 }
		divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_recv = 52 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_display = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_pay tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_pay_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_display = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_gre_recv tooltip = COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_ec_quote_recv_tt }
	}
}
